def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: ZonedDateTime | _: ZonedDateTime => zdt.format(formatter)\n      case ldt: LocalDateTime => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case ld: LocalDate => ld.format(formatter)\n    }\n\n    Token(formatted, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class LocalDateValue(value: LocalDate) extends TemporalToken\n\n  def apply(ldt: LocalDateTime): TemporalToken = LocalDateTimeValue(ldt)\n  def apply(zdt: ZonedDateTime): TemporalToken = ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt)\n  def apply(ld: LocalDate): TemporalToken = LocalDateValue(ld)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Universal Tokenizer - Handles Multiple Types\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass UniversalTokenizer(\n    stringTokenizer: StringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer(),\n    numericTokenizer: NumericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer(),\n    temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, TokenType.STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: String =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n    case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(new String(bytes, stringTokenizer.encoding))\n\n    case int: Int =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(int)\n\n    case float: Float =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(float)\n\n    case double: Double =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(double)\n\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(decimal)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(ldt))\n\n    case zdt: ZonedDateTime =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(zdt))\n\n    case ld: LocalDate =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(ld))\n\n    // Fallback: convert to string\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length (truncated.isEmpty) None else Some(truncated)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.view.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Convenience Functions\n// StringTokenizer(\n    val encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[String] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: String): Token = {\n    val normalized = normalizer(value)\n    Token(normalized, TokenType.STRING)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Numeric Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      val formatted = decimal.setScale(precision, BigDecimal.RoundingMode.HALF_UP)\n      Token(formatted.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"BigDecimal\"))\n    case float: Float =>\n      Token(f\"$float%${precision}.${precision}f\", TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Float\"))\n    case int: Int =>\n      Token(int.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Int\"))\n    case double: Double =>\n      Token(f\"$double%${precision}.${precision}f\", TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Double\"))\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> other.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Temporal Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TemporalTokenizer(\n    formatStr: Option[String] = None\n) extends BaseTokenizer[TemporalToken] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE\n        }\n      }\n\n    val formatted = value match {\n      case TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt) => zdt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue(ldt) => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateValue(ld) => ld.format(formatter)\n    }\n\n    Token(formatted, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class LocalDateValue(value: LocalDate) extends TemporalToken\n\n  def apply(ldt: LocalDateTime): TemporalToken = LocalDateTimeValue(ldt)\n  def apply(zdt: ZonedDateTime): TemporalToken = ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt)\n  def apply(ld: LocalDate): TemporalToken = LocalDateValue(ld)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Universal Tokenizer - Handles Multiple Types\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass UniversalTokenizer(\n    stringTokenizer: StringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer(),\n    numericTokenizer: NumericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer(),\n    temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, TokenType.STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: String =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n    case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(new String(bytes, stringTokenizer.encoding))\n\n    case int: Int =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(int)\n\n    case float: Float =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(float)\n\n    case double: Double =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(double)\n\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(decimal)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(ldt))\n\n    case zdt: ZonedDateTime =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(zdt))\n\n    case ld: LocalDate =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(ld))\n\n    // Fallback: convert to string\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      val truncated = maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) if lowered.length > max => lowered.take(max)\n        case _ => lowered\n      }\n      if (truncated.isEmpty) None else Some(truncated)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.view.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).size\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tokenizer Builder - Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private val normalizers = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[String => String]\n  private val validators = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[T => Boolean]\n  private var metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers += normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators += validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(meta: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ meta\n    this\n  }\n\n  def build(): T => Token = { value: T =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.find(!_(value)).foreach { _ =>\n      throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach { normalizer }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Convenience Functions\n// StringTokenizer(\n    val encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[String] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: String): Token = {\n    val ============================================================================\n\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      val formatted = decimal.setScale(precision, BigDecimal.RoundingMode.HALF_UP)\n      Token(formatted.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"BigDecimal\"))\n    case float: Float =>\n      Token(f\"$float%${precision}.${precision}f\", TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Float\"))\n    case int: Int =>\n      Token(int.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Int\"))\n    case double: Double =>\n      Token(f\"$double%${precision}.${precision}f\", TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Double\"))\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> other.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Temporal Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TemporalTokenizer(\n    formatStr: Option[String] = None\n) extends BaseTokenizer[TemporalToken] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE\n        }\n      }\n\n    val formatted = value match {\n      case TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt) => zdt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue(ldt) => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateValue(ld) => ld.format(formatter)\n    }\n\n    Token(formatted, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends StringTokenizer(),\n    numericTokenizer: NumericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer(),\n    temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def principles\n */\n\nimport scala.util.{Failure, Success, Try}\nimport java.time.{LocalDate, LocalDateTime, ZonedDateTime}\nimport java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter\nimport java.math.{BigDecimal, ============================================================================\n\nobject TokenizerTypes {\n  type StrOrBytes = String\n  type JsonValue = Any\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// TokenType - Sealed Trait Hierarchy\n// ============================================================================\n\nsealed trait TokenType extends Product with Serializable\n\nobject TokenType {\n  case object STRING extends TokenType\n  case object NUMERIC extends TokenType\n  case object TEMPORAL extends TokenType\n  case object STRUCTURED extends TokenType\n  case object BINARY extends TokenType\n  case object NULL extends TokenType\n\n  def fromString(value: String): Option[TokenType] = value match {\n    case \"string\" => Some(STRING)\n    case \"numeric\" => Some(NUMERIC)\n    case \"temporal\" => Some(TEMPORAL)\n    case \"structured\" => Some(STRUCTURED)\n    case \"binary\" => Some(BINARY)\n    case \"null\" => Some(NULL)\n    case _ => None\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Core Token Class - Immutable Case Class\n// ============================================================================\n\ncase class Token(\n    value: String,\n    tokenType: TokenType,\n    metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty[String, Any]\n) {\n  def withMetadata(additionalMetadata: (String, Any)*): Token = {\n    val newMetadata = metadata ++ additionalMetadata\n    copy(metadata = newMetadata)\n  }\n}\n\n// Tokenizable[T] {\n  def toToken(value: T): String\n}\n\nobject Tokenizable {\n  implicit object StringTokenizable extends Tokenizable[String] {\n    def toToken(value: String): String = value\n  }\n\n  implicit def optionTokenizable[T: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[Option[T]] = {\n    new Tokenizable[Option[T]] {\n      def toToken(value: Option[T]): String = value match {\n        case Some(v) => implicitly[Tokenizable[T]].toToken(v)\n        case None => \"NULL\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  implicit def listTokenizable[T: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[List[T]] = {\n    new Tokenizable[List[T]] {\n      def toToken(value: List[T]): String = {\n        val ev = implicitly[Tokenizable[T]]\n        value.map(ev.toToken).mkString(\",\")\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  implicit def mapTokenizable[K: Tokenizable, V: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[Map[K, V]] = {\n    new Tokenizable[Map[K, V]] {\n      def toToken(value: Map[K, V]): String = {\n        val kEv = implicitly[Tokenizable[K]]\n        val class BaseTokenizer[T] {\n  def tokenize(value: T): Token\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[T]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// String Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass StringTokenizer(\n    val encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[String] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: String): Token = {\n    val normalized = normalizer(value)\n    Token(normalized, TokenType.STRING)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Numeric Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      val formatted = decimal.setScale(precision, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)\n      Token(formatted.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"BigDecimal\"))\n    case float: Float =>\n      val formatPattern = s\"%%.${precision}f\"\n      Token(formatPattern.format(float), TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Float\"))\n    case int: Int =>\n      Token(int.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Int\"))\n    case double: Double =>\n      val formatPattern = s\"%%.${precision}f\"\n      Token(formatPattern.format(double), TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Double\"))\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> other.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Temporal Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TemporalTokenizer(\n    formatStr: Option[String] = None\n) extends BaseTokenizer[TemporalToken] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE\n        }\n      }\n\n    val formatted = value match {\n      case TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt) => zdt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue(ldt) => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateValue(ld) => ld.format(formatter)\n    }\n\n    Token(formatted, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends TemporalToken = ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt)\n  def apply(ld: LocalDate): TemporalToken = LocalDateValue(ld)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      val truncated = maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) if lowered.length > max => lowered.take(max)\n        case _ => lowered\n      }\n      if (truncated.isEmpty) None else Some(truncated)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.view.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).size\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tokenizer Builder - Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private val normalizers = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[String => String]\n  private val validators = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[T => Boolean]\n  private var metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers += normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators += validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(meta: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ meta\n    this\n  }\n\n  def build(): T => Token = { value: T =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.find(!_(value)).foreach { _ =>\n      throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach { normalizer =>\n      strValue = normalizer(strValue)\n    }\n\n    Token(strValue, TokenType.STRING, metadata)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Companion Objects and Factory Methods\n// ============================================================================\n\nobject Tokenizer {\n\n  // Global instance for convenience\n  val universal: UniversalTokenizer = new UniversalTokenizer()\n\n  // Factory methods for convenience\n  def stringTokenizer(encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n                      normalizer: String => String = identity): StringTokenizer = {\n    new StringTokenizer(encoding, normalizer)\n  }\n\n  def numericTokenizer(precision: Int = 6,\n                       formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty): NumericTokenizer = {\n    new NumericTokenizer(precision, formatOptions)\n  }\n\n  def temporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None): TemporalTokenizer = {\n    new TemporalTokenizer(formatStr)\n  }\n\n  def whitespaceTokenizer(lowercase: Boolean = false,\n                           minLength: Int = 0,\n                           maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n                           stripPunctuation: Boolean = false): WhitespaceTokenizer = {\n    new WhitespaceTokenizer(lowercase, minLength, maxLength, stripPunctuation)\n  }\n\n  // Type class instances for common types\n  implicit val stringTokenizable: Tokenizable[String] = (value: String) => value\n\n  implicit def optionTokenizable[T: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[Option[T]] = {\n    case Some(value) => value.toToken\n    case None => \"NULL\"\n  }\n\n  implicit def listTokenizable[T: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[List[T]] = {\n    values => values.map(_.toToken).mkString(\",\")\n  }\n\n  implicit def mapTokenizable[K: Tokenizable, V: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[Map[K, V]] = {\n    map => map.map { case (k, v) => s\"${k.toToken}=${v.toToken}\" }.toSeq.sorted.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n Standardized String Tokens\n *\n * This Scala implementation demonstrates:\n * - Immutable data structures\n * - Type safety with generics\n * - Pattern matching for type dispatch\n * - Option/Either for error handling\n * - Case classes and sealed traits\n * - Functional programming principles\n */\n\nimport scala.util.{Failure, Success, Try}\nimport java.time.{LocalDate, LocalDateTime, ============================================================================\n// TokenType - Sealed Trait Hierarchy\n// ============================================================================\n\nsealed trait TokenType extends Product with Serializable\n\nobject TokenType {\n  case object STRING extends TokenType\n TokenType\n\n  def fromString(value: String): Option[TokenType] = value match {\n    case \"string\" => Some(STRING)\n    case \"numeric\" => Some(NUMERIC)\n    case \"temporal\" => Some(TEMPORAL)\n    case \"structured\" => Some(STRUCTURED)\n    case \"binary\" => Some(BINARY)\n    case \"null\" => Some(NULL)\n    case _ => None\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Core Token Class - Immutable Case Class\n// ============================================================================\n\ncase class Token(\n    value: String,\n    tokenType: TokenType,\n    metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty[String, Tokenizable[T] {\n  def toToken(value: T): String\n}\n\nobject Tokenizable {\n  implicit object StringTokenizable extends Tokenizable[String] {\n    def toToken(value: String): String = value\n  }\n\n     def toToken(value: List[T]): String = {\n        val ev = implicitly[Tokenizable[T]]\n        value.map(ev.toToken).mkString(\",\")\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  implicit def mapTokenizable[K: ============================================================================\n// String Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass StringTokenizer(\n    val encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[String] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: String): Token = {\n    val normalized = normalizer(value)\n    Token(normalized, TokenType.STRING)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Numeric Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      val formatted = decimal.setScale(precision, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)\n      Token(formatted.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"BigDecimal\"))\n    case float: Float =>\n      val formatPattern = s\"%%.${precision}f\"\n      Token(formatPattern.format(float), TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Float\"))\n    case int: Int =>\n      Token(int.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Int\"))\n    case double: Double =>\n      val formatPattern = s\"%%.${precision}f\"\n      Token(formatPattern.format(double), TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Double\"))\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> other.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Temporal Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TemporalTokenizer(\n    formatStr: Option[String] = None\n) extends BaseTokenizer[TemporalToken] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE\n        }\n      }\n\n    val formatted = value match {\n      case TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt) => zdt.format(formatter)\n }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends StringTokenizer(),\n    numericTokenizer: NumericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer(),\n    temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n   Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      val truncated = maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) if lowered.length > max => lowered.take(max)\n        case _ => lowered\n      }\n      if (truncated.isEmpty) None else Some(truncated)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.view.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).size\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tokenizer Builder - Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private val normalizers = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[String => String]\n  private val validators = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[T => Boolean]\n  private var metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers += normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators += validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(meta: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ meta\n    this\n  }\n\n  def build(): T => Token = { value: T =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.find(!_(value)).foreach { _ =>\n      throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach { normalizer NumericTokenizer = {\n    new NumericTokenizer(precision, formatOptions)\n  }\n\n  def temporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None): TemporalTokenizer = {\n    new }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Convenience Functions\n// TokenType\n\n  def fromString(value: String): Option[TokenType] = value match {\n    case \"string\" => Some(STRING)\n    case \"numeric\" => Some(NUMERIC)\n    case \"temporal\" => Some(TEMPORAL)\n ============================================================================\n// Core Token Class - Immutable Case Class\n// ============================================================================\n\ncase class Token(\n    value: String,\n    tokenType: TokenType,\n    metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty[String, class BaseTokenizer[T] {\n  def tokenize(value: T): Token\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[T]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// String Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass StringTokenizer(\n    val encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[String] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: String): Token = {\n    val ============================================================================\n\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n      val formatted = decimal.setScale(precision, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)\n      Token(formatted.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"BigDecimal\"))\n    case float: Float =>\n      val formatPattern = s\"%%.${precision}f\"\n      Token(formatPattern.format(float), TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Float\"))\n    case int: Int =>\n      Token(int.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Int\"))\n    case double: Double =>\n      val formatPattern = s\"%%.${precision}f\"\n      Token(formatPattern.format(double), TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Double\"))\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> other.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Temporal Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TemporalTokenizer(\n    formatStr: Option[String] = None\n) extends BaseTokenizer[TemporalToken] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME\n          case _: TemporalToken.LocalDateValue => DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE\n        }\n      }\n\n    val formatted = value match {\n      case TemporalToken.ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt) => zdt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateTimeValue(ldt) => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case TemporalToken.LocalDateValue(ld) => ld.format(formatter)\n    }\n\n    Token(formatted, TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\nsealed trait TemporalToken\n\nobject TemporalToken {\n  case class LocalDateTimeValue(value: LocalDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class ZonedDateTimeValue(value: ZonedDateTime) extends TemporalToken\n  case class LocalDateValue(value: LocalDate) extends TemporalToken\n\n  def apply(ldt: LocalDateTime): TemporalToken = LocalDateTimeValue(ldt)\n  def apply(zdt: ZonedDateTime): TemporalToken = ZonedDateTimeValue(zdt)\n  def apply(ld: LocalDate): TemporalToken = LocalDateValue(ld)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Universal Tokenizer - Handles Multiple Types\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass UniversalTokenizer(\n    stringTokenizer: StringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer(),\n    numericTokenizer: NumericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer(),\n    temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    // Try to use Tokenizable type class first\n    try {\n      val tokenizable = value.asInstanceOf[Tokenizable[Any]]\n      return Token(tokenizable.toToken(value), TokenType.STRUCTURED)\n    } catch {\n      case _: ClassCastException => // Not a Tokenizable\n    }\n\n    value match {\n      case null => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n\n      case str: String =>\n        stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n      case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n        stringTokenizer.tokenize(new String(bytes, stringTokenizer.encoding))\n\n      case int: Int =>\n        numericTokenizer.tokenize(int)\n\n      case float: Float =>\n        numericTokenizer.tokenize(float)\n\n      case double: Double =>\n        numericTokenizer.tokenize(double)\n\n      case decimal: BigDecimal =>\n        numericTokenizer.tokenize(decimal)\n\n      case ldt: LocalDateTime =>\n        temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(ldt))\n\n      case zdt: ZonedDateTime =>\n        temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(zdt))\n\n      case ld: LocalDate =>\n        temporalTokenizer.tokenize(TemporalToken(ld))\n\n      // Fallback: convert to string\n      case other =>\n        Token(other.toString, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      val truncated = maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) if lowered.length > max => lowered.take(max)\n        case _ => lowered\n      }\n      if (truncated.isEmpty) None else Some(truncated)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.view.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).size\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tokenizer Builder - Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private val normalizers = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[String => String]\n  private val validators = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[T => Boolean]\n  private var metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers += normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators += validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(meta: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ meta\n    this\n  }\n\n  def build(): T => Token = { value: T =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.find(!_(value)).foreach { _ =>\n      throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach { normalizer =>\n      strValue = normalizer(strValue)\n    }\n\n    Token(strValue, TokenType.STRING, metadata)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Companion Objects and Factory Methods\n// ============================================================================\n\nobject Tokenizer {\n\n  // Global instance for convenience\n  val universal: UniversalTokenizer = new UniversalTokenizer()\n\n  // Factory methods for convenience\n  def stringTokenizer(encoding: String = \"utf-8\",\n                      normalizer: String => String = NumericTokenizer = {\n    new NumericTokenizer(precision, formatOptions)\n  }\n\n  def temporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None): TemporalTokenizer = {\n    new TemporalTokenizer(formatStr)\n  }\n\n  def whitespaceTokenizer(lowercase: Boolean = false,\n                           minLength: Int = 0,\n                           maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n                           stripPunctuation: Boolean = false): WhitespaceTokenizer = {\n    new WhitespaceTokenizer(lowercase, minLength, maxLength, stripPunctuation)\n  }\n\n  // Type class instances for common types\n  implicit val stringTokenizable: Tokenizable[String] = new Tokenizable[String] {\n    def toToken(value: String): String = value\n  }\n\n  implicit def optionTokenizable[T:        case None => \"NULL\"\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  implicit def listTokenizable[T: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[List[T]] = {\n    new Tokenizable[List[T]] {\n      def toToken(values: List[T]): String = {\n        val ev = implicitly[Tokenizable[T]]\n        values.map(ev.toToken).mkString(\",\")\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  implicit def mapTokenizable[K: Tokenizable, V: Tokenizable]: Tokenizable[Map[K, V]] = {\n    new Tokenizable[Map[K, V]] {\n      def toToken(map: Map[K, V]): String = {\n        val kEv = implicitly[Tokenizable[K]]\n        val vEv = ============================================================================\n// Convenience Functions\n// ============================================================================\n\nobject Tokenize {\n\n  // Single value tokenization\n  def apply(value: Any): Token = Tokenizer.universal.tokenize(value)\n\n  def apply(value: Any, metadata: (String, Any)*): Token = {\n    Tokenizer.universal.tokenize(value).withMetadata(metadata: _*)\n  }\n\n  // Batch tokenization\n  def batch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    Tokenizer.universal.tokenizeBatch(values)\n  }\n\n  def batch(values: Iterable[Any], metadata: (String, Any)*): Iterator[Token] = {\n    batch(values).map(_.withMetadata(metadata: _*))\n  }\n\n  // Convert to token with metadata\n  def BaseTokenizer[TemporalToken] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: TemporalToken): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern)\n      .getOrElse {\n        value ============================================================================\n// Universal Tokenizer - Handles Multiple Types\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass UniversalTokenizer(\n    stringTokenizer: StringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer(),\n    numericTokenizer: NumericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer(),\n    temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    // Try to use Tokenizable type class first\n    try {\n      val tokenizable = value.asInstanceOf[Tokenizable[Any]]\n      return Token(tokenizable.toToken(value), TokenType.STRUCTURED)\n    } catch {\n      case _: ClassCastException => // Not a Tokenizable\n    }\n\n    value match {\n      case null => Token(\"NULL\", TokenType.NULL)\n\n      case str: String =>\n        stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n      case bytes: WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation = Set('.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    } else {\n      word\n    }\n\n    val lowered = if (lowercase) {\n      processed.toLowerCase\n    } else {\n      processed\n    }\n\n    if (lowered.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      val truncated = maxLength match {\n        case Some(max) if lowered.length > max => lowered.take(max)\n        case _ => lowered\n      }\n      if (truncated.isEmpty) None else Some(truncated)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    words.view.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).size\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tokenizer Builder - Fluent Interface\n// validators = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[T => Boolean]\n  private var metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers += normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators += validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(meta: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ meta\n    this\n  }\n\n  def build(): T => Token = { value: T =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.find(!_(value)).foreach { _ =>\n      throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach { normalizer =>\n      strValue = normalizer(strValue)\n    }\n\n    Token(strValue, TokenType.STRING, metadata)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// NumericTokenizer = {\n    new NumericTokenizer(precision, formatOptions)\n  }\n\n  def temporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None): TemporalTokenizer = {\n    new                          stripPunctuation: Boolean = false): WhitespaceTokenizer = {\n    new WhitespaceTokenizer(lowercase, minLength, maxLength, stripPunctuation)\n  }\n\n  // Type class instances Tokenize {\n\n  // Single value tokenization\n  def apply(value: Any): Token = Tokenizer.universal.tokenize(value)\n\n  def apply(value: Any, metadata: (String, Any)*): Token = {\n    Tokenizer.universal.tokenize(value).withMetadata(metadata: _*)\n  }\n\n  // Batch tokenization\n  def batch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    Tokenizer.universal.tokenizeBatch(values)\n
